HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_028c61dc927d7f0c7e24","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:8:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yerleşip kalma boyutu, servetin içinde kendine güvenli bir yurt kurduğunu sanan kişinin son karşısındaki durağan rahatlığını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0d533c009e7442836ee8","text":"92:20-21 supplied the positive replacement for the negated ledger: after no human favor is being repaid, only face-seeking remains, and it ends in رضى. That made this ayah less a disclaimer and more the clearing of the social field for divine relation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000089/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26aecc69ee1ab211fd28","text":"1. **Withholding plus claimed self-sufficiency.** Evidence: `ب خ ل` B001 (stingy withholding), `غ ن ي` B001 (wealth/self-sufficiency). Structural cue: this exactly reverses 92:5’s giving/guarding. Mechanism: the person protects possessions by imagining no need. Abductive move: I infer that self-sufficiency is the inner story that makes withholding coherent. Reading change: miserliness is not just behavior; it rests on a false anthropology of independence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000089/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2f6fdda79b6b94103564","text":"1. **A closed circuit of possession and imagined needlessness.** `بَخِلَ` has the packet’s single branch `ب خ ل:B001` (withholding possessions where they should not be withheld), and `ٱسْتَغْنَىٰ` anchors `غ ن ي:B001` (wealth and self-sufficiency). Their coordination reverses 92:5: outward transfer is replaced by retention, and protective caution by a claim of reduced need. The abductive move is that withholding and imagined independence reinforce one another as a closed circuit; grammar supplies association, not mutual causation. The ayah reads as a self-sealing economy rather than two unrelated faults.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3364f7efb140dab18462","text":"3. **Dwelling and settledness as attachment.** Evidence: `غ ن ي` B004 (settled dwelling/former habitations), activated by 92:11 falling and 92:13 first/last ownership. Structural cue: after self-sufficiency comes loss of support. Mechanism: imagined settled possession is destabilized by temporal ownership belonging to God. Abductive move: I infer that “being at home in wealth” becomes a trap when the end arrives. Reading change: the ayah can imply settled complacency, not merely rich independence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000089/B001","root_001110/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3f97d52b05628a57e35e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:8:3\",\"root_000089\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:8:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Cimrilik ile kendini yeterli sayma birbirini besleyen kapalı bir düzen kurar: dışarıya vermemek, başkasına ihtiyaç olmadığı iddiasını güçlendirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4cdcb3884fb8a88b638e","text":"2. **Sufficing turned into refusal of substitute care.** Evidence: `غ ن ي` B002 (sufficing/availing/replacing), activated by 92:11 “his wealth will not avail” and `ج ز ي` B002 in 92:19 (standing in/sufficing). Structural cue: the root returns negatively in 92:11. Mechanism: the one who declares himself sufficient discovers that his substitute cannot stand in for him. Abductive move: I infer irony from repetition: what he sought as sufficiency becomes non-availing. Reading change: “استغنى” foreshadows collapse of the very sufficiency claimed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_519614b94ee287c1903e","text":"92:11 was decisive: it repeats `غ ن ي` in the availing branch `B002` and explicitly negates the function claimed here. That later test changed `ٱسْتَغْنَىٰ` from a settled achievement into a self-description whose supporting asset fails at the critical moment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c82b8dea6daeda74fbef","text":"2. **Self-sufficiency exposed as a failed functional claim.** The later `يُغْنِى` in 92:11 activates `غ ن ي:B002` (sufficing, availing, or standing in): the same root tests whether possessed wealth can actually perform the sufficiency claimed here. Its explicit failure, after facilitation toward hardship in 92:10, retrospectively corrects `ٱسْتَغْنَىٰ` from achieved status to an assumption awaiting trial. The packet supplies the lexical repetition and negation; the conclusion that 92:11 is the test of 92:8 is abductive. The fixed ayah therefore contains an unstable claim, not a secure condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fbc749b5cbcbcd2ad7ca","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:8:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İleride malın yarar sağlamadığının bildirilmesi, buradaki yeterlilik iddiasını güvenli bir durum değil, kritik anda çökecek bir varsayım hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
